`crates/tuxgate-bridge/src/lib.rs`:

```rs
//! Dual-boot support: find an existing Windows installation and, when the
//! user explicitly confirms, restart into it once.
//!
//! What this crate does not contain, by design: partitioning, formatting,
//! boot manager installation, firmware settings, permanent boot order
//! changes, or any write to the EFI System Partition.

pub mod efi_boot;
pub mod reboot;
pub mod windows;

pub use efi_boot::EfiBootEntry;
pub use reboot::{
    confirmation_matches, execute, plan_reboot, PlannedCommand, RebootMethod, RebootPlan,
    CONFIRMATION_PHRASE,
};
pub use windows::{detect_windows, WindowsDetection};

/// Errors raised when planning or performing a reboot into Windows.
#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum BridgeError {
    #[error("rebooting is only possible on the running system, not on an offline tree")]
    Offline,

    #[error("no Windows installation was detected ({0})")]
    WindowsNotDetected(String),

    #[error("no supported way to boot Windows once was found: {0}")]
    NoSupportedMethod(String),

    #[error("'{program}' is required for this and is not installed (package {package})")]
    MissingTool {
        program: &'static str,
        package: &'static str,
    },

    #[error("'{command}' failed{}", exit_suffix(.code))]
    CommandFailed { command: String, code: Option<i32> },

    #[error("'{command}' could not be run: {reason}")]
    CommandNotRun { command: String, reason: String },

    #[error(
        "Windows was selected for the next boot, but the restart itself failed ({reason}). \
         The next restart will start Windows. To cancel that, run: sudo efibootmgr --delete-bootnext"
    )]
    RebootFailedAfterBootNext { reason: String },
}

fn exit_suffix(code: &Option<i32>) -> String {
    match code {
        Some(code) => format!(" with exit code {code}"),
        None => " (terminated by a signal)".to_string(),
    }
}

```

`crates/tuxgate-bridge/src/reboot.rs`:

```rs
//! Planning and performing a one-time restart into Windows.
//!
//! Planning is pure and read-only. Execution runs at most two commands, in
//! order, and stops at the first failure. Both supported methods affect the
//! next boot only: neither changes the permanent boot order nor writes to
//! the EFI System Partition.
//!
//! The caller is responsible for obtaining the user's confirmation before
//! calling [`execute`]; [`CONFIRMATION_PHRASE`] is what must be typed.

use serde::Serialize;
use tuxgate_core::efi::{self, LOADER_GUID};
use tuxgate_core::{Status, SystemContext};

use crate::windows::WindowsDetection;
use crate::BridgeError;

/// What the user must type to confirm the restart.
pub const CONFIRMATION_PHRASE: &str = "reboot to windows";

/// True when the typed text confirms the restart.
pub fn confirmation_matches(input: &str) -> bool {
    input.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case(CONFIRMATION_PHRASE)
}

/// How Windows will be selected for the next boot.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum RebootMethod {
    /// Ask systemd-boot, through logind, to start one entry once.
    SystemdBootEntry { entry_id: String },
    /// Set the UEFI `BootNext` variable, which the firmware honours once
    /// and then clears.
    EfiBootNext {
        boot_number: String,
        description: String,
    },
}

/// One command of a plan.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct PlannedCommand {
    pub program: String,
    pub args: Vec<String>,
    /// True when the command asks for administrator rights.
    pub elevated: bool,
    pub purpose: String,
}

impl PlannedCommand {
    /// The command as it would be typed.
    pub fn display(&self) -> String {
        std::iter::once(self.program.as_str())
            .chain(self.args.iter().map(String::as_str))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join(" ")
    }
}

/// Everything that will happen, for the user to read before confirming.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct RebootPlan {
    pub method: RebootMethod,
    pub commands: Vec<PlannedCommand>,
    pub warnings: Vec<String>,
}

fn common_warnings() -> Vec<String> {
    vec![
        "The computer restarts immediately. Save your work first.".to_string(),
        "Only the next boot is affected. The permanent boot order is not changed.".to_string(),
        "If Windows uses BitLocker, keep your recovery key at hand: Windows can ask for it \
         after firmware or boot configuration changes."
            .to_string(),
    ]
}

/// Resolves a program to its absolute path in a system directory.
///
/// The user's `PATH` is deliberately ignored here: a command that restarts
/// the machine or runs with administrator rights must not be looked up in
/// a location the user's session can write to.
fn require(
    ctx: &SystemContext,
    program: &'static str,
    package: &'static str,
) -> Result<String, BridgeError> {
    ctx.find_system_program(program)
        .ok_or(BridgeError::MissingTool { program, package })
}

fn running_systemd_boot(ctx: &SystemContext) -> bool {
    efi::read_var(ctx, "LoaderInfo", LOADER_GUID)
        .map(|data| efi::utf16le_to_string(&data))
        .is_ok_and(|info| info.starts_with("systemd-boot"))
}

/// Works out how to start Windows once. Changes nothing.
pub fn plan_reboot(
    ctx: &SystemContext,
    detection: &WindowsDetection,
) -> Result<RebootPlan, BridgeError> {
    if ctx.is_offline() {
        return Err(BridgeError::Offline);
    }
    if detection.status != Status::Detected {
        return Err(BridgeError::WindowsNotDetected(
            detection.status.label().to_string(),
        ));
    }

    // Preferred: systemd-boot started this session and lists Windows. A
    // single command does everything and logind applies the system's own
    // authorisation policy.
    if let Some(entry_id) = &detection.systemd_boot_entry {
        if running_systemd_boot(ctx) {
            let systemctl = require(ctx, "systemctl", "systemd")?;
            return Ok(RebootPlan {
                method: RebootMethod::SystemdBootEntry {
                    entry_id: entry_id.clone(),
                },
                commands: vec![PlannedCommand {
                    program: systemctl,
                    args: vec![
                        "reboot".to_string(),
                        format!("--boot-loader-entry={entry_id}"),
                    ],
                    elevated: false,
                    purpose: "restart and let systemd-boot start Windows once".to_string(),
                }],
                warnings: common_warnings(),
            });
        }
    }

    // Otherwise: the firmware's own one-time selection.
    let Some(entry) = detection.efi_entries.iter().find(|e| e.active) else {
        return Err(BridgeError::NoSupportedMethod(
            "Windows has no active firmware boot entry, and the boot manager in use cannot be \
             asked to start it once. Choose Windows from your boot menu instead."
                .to_string(),
        ));
    };
    let efibootmgr = require(ctx, "efibootmgr", "efibootmgr")?;
    let systemctl = require(ctx, "systemctl", "systemd")?;
    let (elevator_name, elevator) = ["pkexec", "sudo"]
        .into_iter()
        .find_map(|name| ctx.find_system_program(name).map(|path| (name, path)))
        .ok_or(BridgeError::MissingTool {
            program: "pkexec",
            package: "polkit",
        })?;

    let mut warnings = common_warnings();
    warnings.insert(
        1,
        format!(
            "You will be asked for administrator rights by {elevator_name}, for one command: \
             setting the firmware's next-boot entry."
        ),
    );

    Ok(RebootPlan {
        method: RebootMethod::EfiBootNext {
            boot_number: entry.label(),
            description: entry.description.clone(),
        },
        commands: vec![
            PlannedCommand {
                program: elevator,
                args: vec![efibootmgr, "--bootnext".to_string(), entry.label()],
                elevated: true,
                purpose: format!(
                    "select firmware entry Boot{} for the next boot only",
                    entry.label()
                ),
            },
            PlannedCommand {
                program: systemctl,
                args: vec!["reboot".to_string()],
                elevated: false,
                purpose: "restart".to_string(),
            },
        ],
        warnings,
    })
}

/// Runs a plan. Call only after the user has confirmed.
///
/// Stops at the first command that fails; a failed first step means the
/// restart is not attempted.
pub fn execute(ctx: &SystemContext, plan: &RebootPlan) -> Result<(), BridgeError> {
    if ctx.is_offline() {
        return Err(BridgeError::Offline);
    }
    let boot_next_method = matches!(plan.method, RebootMethod::EfiBootNext { .. });

    for (index, command) in plan.commands.iter().enumerate() {
        let args: Vec<&str> = command.args.iter().map(String::as_str).collect();
        let is_final_restart = boot_next_method && index > 0;

        let failure = match ctx.run_interactive(&command.program, &args) {
            Ok(Some(0)) => continue,
            Ok(code) => BridgeError::CommandFailed {
                command: command.display(),
                code,
            },
            Err(error) => BridgeError::CommandNotRun {
                command: command.display(),
                reason: error.to_string(),
            },
        };

        // BootNext is already set at this point: tell the user, and how to
        // undo it.
        return Err(if is_final_restart {
            BridgeError::RebootFailedAfterBootNext {
                reason: failure.to_string(),
            }
        } else {
            failure
        });
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::detect_windows;
    use tuxgate_core::efi::{var_path, EFI_GLOBAL_GUID};
    use tuxgate_core::testing::{efi_load_option, efi_var_u8, scenarios, Fixture, MockRunner};

    /// A GRUB machine with a Windows firmware entry and the needed tools.
    fn grub_dual_boot(tools: &[&str]) -> Fixture {
        let mut fixture = Fixture::new()
            .bytes(&var_path("SecureBoot", EFI_GLOBAL_GUID), &efi_var_u8(0))
            .bytes(
                &var_path("Boot0002", EFI_GLOBAL_GUID),
                &efi_load_option(
                    "Windows Boot Manager",
                    Some("\\EFI\\Microsoft\\Boot\\bootmgfw.efi"),
                ),
            )
            .bytes(
                &var_path("Boot0005", EFI_GLOBAL_GUID),
                &efi_load_option("GRUB", Some("\\EFI\\GRUB\\grubx64.efi")),
            )
            .file("/boot/grub/grub.cfg", "");
        for tool in tools {
            fixture = fixture.file(&format!("/usr/bin/{tool}"), "");
        }
        fixture
    }

    fn plan_for(fixture: &Fixture, runner: MockRunner) -> Result<RebootPlan, BridgeError> {
        let ctx = fixture.context_with(runner, &[("PATH", "/usr/bin")]);
        plan_reboot(&ctx, &detect_windows(&ctx))
    }

    #[test]
    fn confirmation_requires_the_exact_phrase() {
        assert!(confirmation_matches("reboot to windows"));
        assert!(confirmation_matches("  Reboot To Windows\n"));
        for rejected in [
            "",
            "y",
            "yes",
            "reboot",
            "reboot to window",
            "reboot to windows now",
        ] {
            assert!(
                !confirmation_matches(rejected),
                "{rejected:?} must not confirm"
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn systemd_boot_plan_is_a_single_unprivileged_command() {
        let scenario = scenarios::cachyos_desktop();
        let ctx = scenario.context();
        let plan = plan_reboot(&ctx, &detect_windows(&ctx)).expect("plan");
        assert_eq!(
            plan.method,
            RebootMethod::SystemdBootEntry {
                entry_id: "auto-windows".to_string()
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(plan.commands.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(
            plan.commands[0].display(),
            "/usr/bin/systemctl reboot --boot-loader-entry=auto-windows"
        );
        assert!(!plan.commands[0].elevated);
        assert!(plan.warnings.iter().any(|w| w.contains("Save your work")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn grub_machine_uses_boot_next_through_pkexec() {
        let fixture = grub_dual_boot(&["efibootmgr", "systemctl", "pkexec", "sudo"]);
        let plan = plan_for(&fixture, MockRunner::new()).expect("plan");
        assert_eq!(
            plan.method,
            RebootMethod::EfiBootNext {
                boot_number: "0002".to_string(),
                description: "Windows Boot Manager".to_string()
            }
        );
        let lines: Vec<_> = plan.commands.iter().map(PlannedCommand::display).collect();
        assert_eq!(
            lines,
            [
                "/usr/bin/pkexec /usr/bin/efibootmgr --bootnext 0002",
                "/usr/bin/systemctl reboot"
            ]
        );
        assert!(plan.commands[0].elevated);
        assert!(!plan.commands[1].elevated);
        assert!(plan
            .warnings
            .iter()
            .any(|w| w.contains("administrator rights")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn falls_back_to_sudo_without_pkexec() {
        let fixture = grub_dual_boot(&["efibootmgr", "systemctl", "sudo"]);
        let plan = plan_for(&fixture, MockRunner::new()).expect("plan");
        assert_eq!(
            plan.commands[0].display(),
            "/usr/bin/sudo /usr/bin/efibootmgr --bootnext 0002"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_tools_are_named_with_their_package() {
        let no_efibootmgr = grub_dual_boot(&["systemctl", "pkexec"]);
        assert_eq!(
            plan_for(&no_efibootmgr, MockRunner::new()),
            Err(BridgeError::MissingTool {
                program: "efibootmgr",
                package: "efibootmgr"
            })
        );
        let no_elevation = grub_dual_boot(&["efibootmgr", "systemctl"]);
        assert_eq!(
            plan_for(&no_elevation, MockRunner::new()),
            Err(BridgeError::MissingTool {
                program: "pkexec",
                package: "polkit"
            })
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_program_planted_in_the_users_path_is_never_used() {
        // A fake efibootmgr earlier in PATH, and no real one: the plan must
        // fail rather than hand the fake to pkexec.
        let hijacked = grub_dual_boot(&["systemctl", "pkexec"]).file("/home/u/bin/efibootmgr", "");
        let ctx = hijacked.context_with(MockRunner::new(), &[("PATH", "/home/u/bin:/usr/bin")]);
        assert_eq!(
            plan_reboot(&ctx, &detect_windows(&ctx)),
            Err(BridgeError::MissingTool {
                program: "efibootmgr",
                package: "efibootmgr"
            })
        );

        // With the real one present, the system copy is used by absolute path.
        let both = grub_dual_boot(&["efibootmgr", "systemctl", "pkexec"])
            .file("/home/u/bin/efibootmgr", "")
            .file("/home/u/bin/pkexec", "")
            .file("/home/u/bin/systemctl", "");
        let ctx = both.context_with(MockRunner::new(), &[("PATH", "/home/u/bin:/usr/bin")]);
        let plan = plan_reboot(&ctx, &detect_windows(&ctx)).expect("plan");
        for command in &plan.commands {
            assert!(
                command.program.starts_with("/usr/bin/"),
                "{}",
                command.display()
            );
            assert!(
                !command.display().contains("/home/u/bin"),
                "{}",
                command.display()
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn no_plan_without_windows() {
        let scenario = scenarios::arch_minimal();
        let ctx = scenario.context();
        assert_eq!(
            plan_reboot(&ctx, &detect_windows(&ctx)),
            Err(BridgeError::WindowsNotDetected("not detected".to_string()))
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn no_plan_when_windows_is_only_a_file_on_the_esp() {
        // Found on the ESP, but no firmware entry and not systemd-boot.
        let fixture = Fixture::new()
            .dir("/sys/firmware/efi/efivars")
            .file("/boot/efi/EFI/Microsoft/Boot/bootmgfw.efi", "")
            .file("/usr/bin/efibootmgr", "")
            .file("/usr/bin/systemctl", "");
        assert!(matches!(
            plan_for(&fixture, MockRunner::new()),
            Err(BridgeError::NoSupportedMethod(_))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn offline_trees_cannot_be_rebooted() {
        let scenario = scenarios::cachyos_desktop();
        let live = scenario.context();
        let detection = detect_windows(&live);
        let plan = plan_reboot(&live, &detection).expect("plan");

        let offline = SystemContext::offline(scenario.fixture.root());
        assert_eq!(plan_reboot(&offline, &detection), Err(BridgeError::Offline));
        assert_eq!(execute(&offline, &plan), Err(BridgeError::Offline));
    }

    #[test]
    fn planning_runs_no_state_changing_command() {
        let fixture = grub_dual_boot(&["efibootmgr", "systemctl", "pkexec"]);
        let runner = MockRunner::new();
        plan_for(&fixture, runner.clone()).expect("plan");
        for call in runner.calls() {
            assert!(
                !call.starts_with("interactive:"),
                "planning must not run interactive commands: {call}"
            );
            assert!(
                call.starts_with("bootctl list") || call.starts_with("lsblk "),
                "unexpected command while planning: {call}"
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn executes_commands_in_order() {
        let fixture = grub_dual_boot(&["efibootmgr", "systemctl", "pkexec"]);
        let runner = MockRunner::new()
            .on_interactive("/usr/bin/pkexec /usr/bin/efibootmgr --bootnext 0002", 0)
            .on_interactive("/usr/bin/systemctl reboot", 0);
        let ctx = fixture.context_with(runner.clone(), &[("PATH", "/usr/bin")]);
        let plan = plan_reboot(&ctx, &detect_windows(&ctx)).expect("plan");
        execute(&ctx, &plan).expect("execute");

        let interactive: Vec<_> = runner
            .calls()
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|c| c.starts_with("interactive:"))
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(
            interactive,
            [
                "interactive: /usr/bin/pkexec /usr/bin/efibootmgr --bootnext 0002",
                "interactive: /usr/bin/systemctl reboot"
            ]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_refused_elevation_stops_before_the_restart() {
        let fixture = grub_dual_boot(&["efibootmgr", "systemctl", "pkexec"]);
        // pkexec exits 126 when the user dismisses the authentication dialog.
        let runner = MockRunner::new()
            .on_interactive("/usr/bin/pkexec /usr/bin/efibootmgr --bootnext 0002", 126)
            .on_interactive("/usr/bin/systemctl reboot", 0);
        let ctx = fixture.context_with(runner.clone(), &[("PATH", "/usr/bin")]);
        let plan = plan_reboot(&ctx, &detect_windows(&ctx)).expect("plan");

        assert_eq!(
            execute(&ctx, &plan),
            Err(BridgeError::CommandFailed {
                command: "/usr/bin/pkexec /usr/bin/efibootmgr --bootnext 0002".to_string(),
                code: Some(126)
            })
        );
        assert!(!runner
            .calls()
            .iter()
            .any(|c| c == "interactive: /usr/bin/systemctl reboot"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_failed_restart_after_boot_next_explains_how_to_undo() {
        let fixture = grub_dual_boot(&["efibootmgr", "systemctl", "pkexec"]);
        let runner = MockRunner::new()
            .on_interactive("/usr/bin/pkexec /usr/bin/efibootmgr --bootnext 0002", 0)
            .on_interactive("/usr/bin/systemctl reboot", 1);
        let ctx = fixture.context_with(runner, &[("PATH", "/usr/bin")]);
        let plan = plan_reboot(&ctx, &detect_windows(&ctx)).expect("plan");

        let error = execute(&ctx, &plan).expect_err("restart fails");
        assert!(matches!(
            error,
            BridgeError::RebootFailedAfterBootNext { .. }
        ));
        assert!(error.to_string().contains("efibootmgr --delete-bootnext"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_failed_systemd_boot_restart_is_a_plain_failure() {
        let scenario = scenarios::cachyos_desktop();
        let runner = scenario.runner.clone().on_interactive(
            "/usr/bin/systemctl reboot --boot-loader-entry=auto-windows",
            1,
        );
        let ctx = scenario.fixture.context_with(runner, &scenario.env);
        let plan = plan_reboot(&ctx, &detect_windows(&ctx)).expect("plan");
        assert!(matches!(
            execute(&ctx, &plan),
            Err(BridgeError::CommandFailed { code: Some(1), .. })
        ));
    }
}
```

`crates/tuxgate-bridge/src/windows.rs`:

```rs
//! Detection of an existing Windows installation. Read-only.
//!
//! Three independent sources are consulted, strongest first: the firmware
//! boot entries, the Windows boot manager file on the EFI System Partition,
//! and systemd-boot's own entry list. Partition types are only a hint.

use serde::Serialize;
use tuxgate_core::efi;
use tuxgate_core::text::sanitize;
use tuxgate_core::{CommandError, FsError, Status, SystemContext};

use crate::efi_boot::{self, EfiBootEntry};

/// Mount points where the EFI System Partition is commonly found.
const ESP_MOUNT_POINTS: [&str; 3] = ["/boot", "/boot/efi", "/efi"];

/// Relative path of the Windows boot manager on the ESP.
const WINDOWS_LOADER_FILE: &str = "EFI/Microsoft/Boot/bootmgfw.efi";

/// What was found about Windows on this machine.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct WindowsDetection {
    pub status: Status,
    /// Firmware boot entries that start Windows.
    pub efi_entries: Vec<EfiBootEntry>,
    /// Location of the Windows boot manager on the ESP, when visible.
    pub esp_loader: Option<String>,
    /// Identifier of the Windows entry in systemd-boot, when listed.
    pub systemd_boot_entry: Option<String>,
    /// Partitions that look like they belong to Windows. A hint only.
    pub partition_hints: Vec<String>,
    /// Files read and commands run.
    pub evidence: Vec<String>,
    pub notes: Vec<String>,
}

impl WindowsDetection {
    /// One-line summary.
    pub fn summary(&self) -> String {
        match self.status {
            Status::Detected => {
                let mut parts = Vec::new();
                if let Some(entry) = self.efi_entries.first() {
                    parts.push(format!(
                        "firmware entry Boot{} \"{}\"",
                        entry.label(),
                        entry.description
                    ));
                }
                if let Some(id) = &self.systemd_boot_entry {
                    parts.push(format!("systemd-boot entry \"{id}\""));
                }
                if let Some(path) = &self.esp_loader {
                    parts.push(format!("boot manager at {path}"));
                }
                format!("Windows installation found: {}", parts.join(", "))
            }
            Status::NotDetected => "No Windows installation was found.".to_string(),
            _ => "Whether Windows is installed could not be determined.".to_string(),
        }
    }
}

/// Finds the Windows entry in `bootctl list --json=short` output.
///
/// systemd-boot names its automatically discovered Windows entry
/// `auto-windows`; a title match covers manually written entries.
pub fn windows_entry_in_bootctl_json(json: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let entries: Vec<serde_json::Value> = serde_json::from_str(json).ok()?;
    entries.iter().find_map(|entry| {
        let id = entry.get("id")?.as_str()?;
        let title = entry.get("title").and_then(|t| t.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
        (id.starts_with("auto-windows") || title.to_lowercase().contains("windows"))
            .then(|| sanitize(id))
    })
}

/// Collects partitions that look like Windows from `lsblk -J` output.
pub fn windows_partitions_in_lsblk_json(json: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    fn walk(node: &serde_json::Value, found: &mut Vec<String>) {
        let text = |key: &str| node.get(key).and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
        let name = text("name");
        let fstype = text("fstype");
        let label = text("partlabel");
        let hint = if fstype.eq_ignore_ascii_case("bitlocker") {
            Some("BitLocker volume")
        } else if label.eq_ignore_ascii_case("Microsoft reserved partition") {
            Some("Microsoft reserved partition")
        } else if fstype.eq_ignore_ascii_case("ntfs") {
            Some("NTFS")
        } else {
            None
        };
        if let (Some(hint), false) = (hint, name.is_empty()) {
            found.push(format!("{} ({hint})", sanitize(name)));
        }
        if let Some(children) = node.get("children").and_then(|c| c.as_array()) {
            for child in children {
                walk(child, found);
            }
        }
    }

    let mut found = Vec::new();
    if let Ok(root) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(json) {
        if let Some(devices) = root.get("blockdevices").and_then(|d| d.as_array()) {
            for device in devices {
                walk(device, &mut found);
            }
        }
    }
    found
}

/// Looks for Windows. Never modifies anything and never asks for privileges.
pub fn detect_windows(ctx: &SystemContext) -> WindowsDetection {
    let mut detection = WindowsDetection {
        status: Status::Unverifiable,
        efi_entries: Vec::new(),
        esp_loader: None,
        systemd_boot_entry: None,
        partition_hints: Vec::new(),
        evidence: Vec::new(),
        notes: Vec::new(),
    };

    // 1. Firmware boot entries.
    let uefi = efi::booted_with_uefi(ctx);
    let mut firmware_readable = false;
    if uefi {
        match efi_boot::list_boot_entries(ctx) {
            Ok(entries) => {
                firmware_readable = true;
                detection.evidence.push(efi::EFIVARS_DIR.to_string());
                detection.efi_entries = entries
                    .into_iter()
                    .filter(EfiBootEntry::is_windows)
                    .collect();
            }
            Err(error) => detection
                .notes
                .push(format!("UEFI boot entries could not be read: {error}.")),
        }
    } else {
        detection.notes.push(
            "The system was not booted through UEFI, so firmware boot entries are not available."
                .to_string(),
        );
    }

    // 2. The Windows boot manager on the ESP.
    let mut esp_visible = false;
    let mut esp_denied = false;
    for mount in ESP_MOUNT_POINTS {
        let candidate = format!("{mount}/{WINDOWS_LOADER_FILE}");
        match ctx.stat(&candidate) {
            Ok(_) => {
                detection.evidence.push(candidate.clone());
                detection.esp_loader = Some(candidate);
                esp_visible = true;
                break;
            }
            Err(FsError::PermissionDenied) => esp_denied = true,
            Err(_) => {
                // Distinguish "ESP readable, no Windows there" from "ESP
                // not listable".
                match ctx.list_dir(&format!("{mount}/EFI")) {
                    Ok(_) => esp_visible = true,
                    Err(FsError::PermissionDenied) => esp_denied = true,
                    Err(_) => {}
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if esp_denied && detection.esp_loader.is_none() {
        detection.notes.push(
            "The EFI System Partition is not readable without administrator rights, so the \
             Windows boot manager file could not be looked for."
                .to_string(),
        );
    }

    // 3. systemd-boot's entry list.
    match ctx.run("bootctl", &["list", "--json=short"]) {
        Ok(output) if output.success() => {
            detection
                .evidence
                .push("bootctl list --json=short".to_string());
            detection.systemd_boot_entry = windows_entry_in_bootctl_json(&output.stdout);
        }
        Ok(_) => detection.notes.push(
            "bootctl could not list boot entries (this is normal when the ESP is not \
             readable or systemd-boot is not installed)."
                .to_string(),
        ),
        Err(CommandError::NotFound | CommandError::Disabled) => {}
        Err(error) => detection.notes.push(format!("bootctl: {error}.")),
    }

    // 4. Partition hints. Never enough on their own.
    if let Ok(output) = ctx.run("lsblk", &["-J", "-o", "NAME,FSTYPE,PARTLABEL"]) {
        if output.success() {
            detection
                .evidence
                .push("lsblk -J -o NAME,FSTYPE,PARTLABEL".to_string());
            detection.partition_hints = windows_partitions_in_lsblk_json(&output.stdout);
        }
    }

    let found = !detection.efi_entries.is_empty()
        || detection.esp_loader.is_some()
        || detection.systemd_boot_entry.is_some();

    detection.status = if found {
        Status::Detected
    } else if firmware_readable || esp_visible {
        Status::NotDetected
    } else {
        Status::Unverifiable
    };

    if !found && !detection.partition_hints.is_empty() {
        detection.notes.push(format!(
            "Partitions that may belong to Windows exist ({}), but no Windows boot entry was \
             found. They may be data disks.",
            detection.partition_hints.join(", ")
        ));
    }
    if detection.status == Status::NotDetected {
        detection.notes.push(
            "Tuxgate remains fully usable without Windows; only `tuxgate windows reboot` \
             needs it."
                .to_string(),
        );
    }
    detection
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use tuxgate_core::testing::{scenarios, Fixture, MockRunner};

    #[test]
    fn dual_boot_is_detected_from_three_sources() {
        let scenario = scenarios::cachyos_desktop();
        let detection = detect_windows(&scenario.context());
        assert_eq!(detection.status, Status::Detected);
        assert_eq!(detection.efi_entries.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(detection.efi_entries[0].label(), "0000");
        assert_eq!(
            detection.esp_loader.as_deref(),
            Some("/boot/EFI/Microsoft/Boot/bootmgfw.efi")
        );
        assert_eq!(
            detection.systemd_boot_entry.as_deref(),
            Some("auto-windows")
        );
        assert!(detection.summary().contains("Boot0000"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn linux_only_machine_is_not_detected() {
        let scenario = scenarios::arch_minimal();
        let detection = detect_windows(&scenario.context());
        assert_eq!(detection.status, Status::NotDetected);
        assert!(detection.efi_entries.is_empty());
        assert!(detection.notes.iter().any(|n| n.contains("fully usable")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn bios_machine_is_unverifiable() {
        let scenario = scenarios::vm_guest();
        let detection = detect_windows(&scenario.context());
        assert_eq!(detection.status, Status::Unverifiable);
        assert!(detection
            .notes
            .iter()
            .any(|n| n.contains("not booted through UEFI")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn ntfs_partitions_alone_do_not_count_as_windows() {
        let scenario = scenarios::arch_minimal();
        let runner = MockRunner::new().on(
            "lsblk -J -o NAME,FSTYPE,PARTLABEL",
            r#"{"blockdevices":[{"name":"sda","fstype":null,"partlabel":null,"children":[{"name":"sda1","fstype":"ntfs","partlabel":"Basic data partition"}]}]}"#,
        );
        let ctx = scenario.fixture.context_with(runner, &scenario.env);
        let detection = detect_windows(&ctx);
        assert_eq!(detection.status, Status::NotDetected);
        assert_eq!(detection.partition_hints, ["sda1 (NTFS)"]);
        assert!(detection
            .notes
            .iter()
            .any(|n| n.contains("may be data disks")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn esp_loader_alone_is_enough() {
        let fixture = Fixture::new().file("/boot/efi/EFI/Microsoft/Boot/bootmgfw.efi", "");
        let detection = detect_windows(&fixture.context());
        assert_eq!(detection.status, Status::Detected);
        assert!(detection.efi_entries.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_bootctl_json() {
        assert_eq!(
            windows_entry_in_bootctl_json(scenarios::BOOTCTL_LIST_WITH_WINDOWS).as_deref(),
            Some("auto-windows")
        );
        assert_eq!(
            windows_entry_in_bootctl_json(r#"[{"id":"win.conf","title":"Windows 11"}]"#).as_deref(),
            Some("win.conf")
        );
        assert_eq!(
            windows_entry_in_bootctl_json(r#"[{"id":"linux.conf","title":"CachyOS"}]"#),
            None
        );
        assert_eq!(windows_entry_in_bootctl_json("not json"), None);
        assert_eq!(windows_entry_in_bootctl_json("{}"), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_lsblk_json() {
        let json = r#"{"blockdevices":[
            {"name":"nvme0n1","fstype":null,"partlabel":null,"children":[
                {"name":"nvme0n1p1","fstype":"vfat","partlabel":"EFI system partition"},
                {"name":"nvme0n1p2","fstype":null,"partlabel":"Microsoft reserved partition"},
                {"name":"nvme0n1p3","fstype":"BitLocker","partlabel":"Basic data partition"},
                {"name":"nvme0n1p4","fstype":"btrfs","partlabel":"root"}
            ]}]}"#;
        assert_eq!(
            windows_partitions_in_lsblk_json(json),
            [
                "nvme0n1p2 (Microsoft reserved partition)",
                "nvme0n1p3 (BitLocker volume)"
            ]
        );
        assert!(windows_partitions_in_lsblk_json("garbage").is_empty());
    }
}
```

`crates/tuxgate-cli/src/app.rs`:

```rs
//! Shared state of a command invocation: configuration, the system being
//! inspected, the compatibility database and output settings.

use std::io::{IsTerminal, Write};

use tuxgate_compatibility::Compatibility;
use tuxgate_core::config::ColorMode;
use tuxgate_core::{Config, Paths, SystemContext};

use crate::cli::Cli;

/// An error to print before exiting with status 1.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct CliError(pub String);

impl std::fmt::Display for CliError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(&self.0)
    }
}

macro_rules! cli_error_from {
    ($($source:ty),+ $(,)?) => {
        $(impl From<$source> for CliError {
            fn from(error: $source) -> Self {
                CliError(error.to_string())
            }
        })+
    };
}

cli_error_from!(
    tuxgate_core::CoreError,
    tuxgate_compatibility::CompatError,
    tuxgate_bridge::BridgeError,
    tuxgate_reporting::ReportError,
    std::io::Error,
    serde_json::Error,
);

/// Result type of every command.
pub type CliResult = Result<(), CliError>;

/// Everything a command needs.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct App {
    pub ctx: SystemContext,
    pub config: Config,
    pub compatibility: Compatibility,
    /// User directories; absent when HOME is unknown.
    pub paths: Option<Paths>,
    pub json: bool,
    pub color: bool,
}

impl App {
    /// Builds the application state from the command line.
    pub fn new(cli: &Cli) -> Result<Self, CliError> {
        let paths = Paths::from_process_env().ok();

        let config = match (&cli.config, &paths) {
            (Some(file), _) => {
                // An explicitly named file must exist.
                if !file.is_file() {
                    return Err(CliError(format!(
                        "configuration file {} does not exist",
                        file.display()
                    )));
                }
                Config::load(file)?
            }
            (None, Some(paths)) => Config::load(&paths.config_file())?,
            (None, None) => Config::default(),
        };

        tuxgate_diagnostics::default_registry().validate_disabled(&config.probes.disabled)?;

        let ctx = match &cli.root {
            Some(root) => {
                if !root.is_dir() {
                    return Err(CliError(format!(
                        "--root {} is not a directory",
                        root.display()
                    )));
                }
                SystemContext::offline(root)
            }
            None => SystemContext::host(),
        }
        .with_command_timeout(config.command_timeout());

        let color = !cli.json
            && !cli.no_color
            && match config.output.color {
                ColorMode::Always => true,
                ColorMode::Never => false,
                ColorMode::Auto => {
                    std::io::stdout().is_terminal()
                        && std::env::var_os("NO_COLOR").is_none_or(|v| v.is_empty())
                }
            };

        Ok(Self {
            ctx,
            config,
            compatibility: Compatibility::builtin()?,
            paths,
            json: cli.json,
            color,
        })
    }

    /// The user directories, or an explanation of why they are unknown.
    pub fn require_paths(&self) -> Result<&Paths, CliError> {
        self.paths.as_ref().ok_or_else(|| {
            CliError("the user data directory is unknown because HOME is not set".to_string())
        })
    }
}

/// Writes to standard output. A closed pipe (`tuxgate doctor | head`) ends
/// the output quietly instead of raising an error.
pub fn emit(text: &str) -> CliResult {
    let mut stdout = std::io::stdout().lock();
    let result = stdout
        .write_all(text.as_bytes())
        .and_then(|()| {
            if text.ends_with('\n') {
                Ok(())
            } else {
                stdout.write_all(b"\n")
            }
        })
        .and_then(|()| stdout.flush());
    match result {
        Err(error) if error.kind() != std::io::ErrorKind::BrokenPipe => Err(error.into()),
        _ => Ok(()),
    }
}

/// Serialises a value as pretty JSON on standard output.
pub fn emit_json<T: serde::Serialize>(value: &T) -> CliResult {
    emit(&serde_json::to_string_pretty(value)?)
}

```

`crates/tuxgate-cli/src/cli.rs`:

```rs
//! Command-line grammar.
//!
//! There is deliberately no command to install, enable or configure
//! Vanguard: no such thing is possible on Linux.

use std::path::PathBuf;

use clap::{Parser, Subcommand, ValueEnum};

const ABOUT: &str = "Unofficial companion for Riot Games titles on Linux";

const LONG_ABOUT: &str = "\
Tuxgate diagnoses your Linux system, documents which Riot Games titles can run on it and why, \
and helps with dual boot.

It does NOT install, emulate, bypass or interact with Riot Vanguard or any other anti-cheat. \
Vanguard has no Linux version; nothing in this program changes that.

Tuxgate is an independent project, not affiliated with Riot Games or CachyOS.";

const AFTER_HELP: &str = "\
Tuxgate never sends data anywhere and never needs administrator rights, except for the single \
confirmed step of `tuxgate windows reboot` on systems without systemd-boot.

Documentation: README.md, RESEARCH.md and SECURITY.md in the project repository.";

/// Top-level arguments.
#[derive(Debug, Parser)]
#[command(
    name = "tuxgate",
    version,
    about = ABOUT,
    long_about = LONG_ABOUT,
    after_help = AFTER_HELP,
    propagate_version = true
)]
pub struct Cli {
    /// Print machine-readable JSON instead of text
    #[arg(long, global = true)]
    pub json: bool,

    /// Disable coloured output
    #[arg(long, global = true)]
    pub no_color: bool,

    /// Inspect a system tree mounted at DIR instead of the running system
    /// (no command is executed in this mode)
    #[arg(long, global = true, value_name = "DIR")]
    pub root: Option<PathBuf>,

    /// Use FILE instead of ~/.config/tuxgate/config.toml
    #[arg(long, global = true, value_name = "FILE")]
    pub config: Option<PathBuf>,

    #[command(subcommand)]
    pub command: Command,
}

/// Subcommands.
#[derive(Debug, Subcommand)]
pub enum Command {
    /// Run every diagnostic and show the full result
    Doctor {
        /// Also show the files read and commands run for each finding
        #[arg(long, short)]
        verbose: bool,
    },

    /// Show a short overview of the system
    Status,

    /// List Riot titles and their documented status, or manage your own test records
    Games {
        #[command(subcommand)]
        action: Option<GamesAction>,
    },

    /// Explain in detail whether a title can run on this system
    Check {
        /// Title identifier, alias or name (see `tuxgate games`)
        game: String,
    },

    /// Produce a local technical report (printed unless --output is given)
    Report {
        /// Output format
        #[arg(long, value_enum, default_value_t = FormatArg::Text)]
        format: FormatArg,

        /// Write the report to FILE instead of printing it
        #[arg(long, short, value_name = "FILE")]
        output: Option<PathBuf>,

        /// Keep user paths and identifiers in the report
        #[arg(long, conflicts_with = "anonymize")]
        no_anonymize: bool,

        /// Remove user paths and identifiers even if the configuration disables it
        #[arg(long)]
        anonymize: bool,

        /// Overwrite FILE if it exists
        #[arg(long, requires = "output")]
        force: bool,
    },

    /// Detect Windows, or restart into it once
    Windows {
        #[command(subcommand)]
        action: WindowsAction,
    },

    /// List the sources behind the compatibility database
    Sources,

    /// List the diagnostic probes and whether they are enabled
    Probes,

    /// Print a shell completion script
    Completions {
        #[arg(value_enum)]
        shell: clap_complete::Shell,
    },

    /// Print the manual page in roff format
    #[command(hide = true)]
    Man,
}

/// `tuxgate games` actions.
#[derive(Debug, Subcommand)]
pub enum GamesAction {
    /// List titles and their documented status (the default)
    List,

    /// Show your recorded test results and any regression
    History {
        /// Restrict to one title
        game: Option<String>,
    },

    /// Record what you observed when you tried a title
    Record {
        /// Title identifier, alias or name
        game: String,

        /// What happened
        #[arg(long, value_enum)]
        result: ResultArg,

        /// How the game was run, for example "Proton 10.0"
        #[arg(long, value_name = "TEXT")]
        runner: Option<String>,

        /// Free-text note (500 characters at most)
        #[arg(long, value_name = "TEXT")]
        note: Option<String>,
    },
}

/// `tuxgate windows` actions.
#[derive(Debug, Subcommand)]
pub enum WindowsAction {
    /// Look for an existing Windows installation (read-only)
    Detect,

    /// Restart into Windows once, after showing the plan and asking for confirmation
    Reboot {
        /// Show what would be done and stop
        #[arg(long)]
        dry_run: bool,
    },
}

/// Report formats.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, ValueEnum)]
pub enum FormatArg {
    Text,
    Markdown,
    Json,
}

/// Test outcomes accepted by `games record`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, ValueEnum)]
pub enum ResultArg {
    /// The game started and was playable
    Works,
    /// The game failed, but not because of an anti-cheat refusal
    Broken,
    /// The anti-cheat or the vendor refused the system
    Blocked,
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use clap::CommandFactory;

    #[test]
    fn grammar_is_consistent() {
        Cli::command().debug_assert();
    }

    #[test]
    fn no_command_pretends_to_set_up_vanguard() {
        let command = Cli::command();
        let mut names = Vec::new();
        fn collect(command: &clap::Command, names: &mut Vec<String>) {
            for sub in command.get_subcommands() {
                names.push(sub.get_name().to_string());
                collect(sub, names);
            }
        }
        collect(&command, &mut names);
        for forbidden in [
            "install", "enable", "vanguard", "activate", "bypass", "spoof", "patch",
        ] {
            assert!(
                !names.iter().any(|n| n.contains(forbidden)),
                "a subcommand is named after '{forbidden}'"
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn help_states_the_limits() {
        let help = Cli::command().render_long_help().to_string();
        assert!(help.contains("does NOT install, emulate, bypass"));
        assert!(help.contains("not affiliated with Riot Games"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_typical_invocations() {
        let cli = Cli::try_parse_from(["tuxgate", "check", "valorant", "--json"]).expect("parse");
        assert!(cli.json);
        assert!(matches!(cli.command, Command::Check { ref game } if game == "valorant"));

        let cli =
            Cli::try_parse_from(["tuxgate", "windows", "reboot", "--dry-run"]).expect("parse");
        assert!(matches!(
            cli.command,
            Command::Windows {
                action: WindowsAction::Reboot { dry_run: true }
            }
        ));

        let cli = Cli::try_parse_from(["tuxgate", "games"]).expect("parse");
        assert!(matches!(cli.command, Command::Games { action: None }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_contradictory_report_options() {
        assert!(
            Cli::try_parse_from(["tuxgate", "report", "--anonymize", "--no-anonymize"]).is_err()
        );
        assert!(Cli::try_parse_from(["tuxgate", "report", "--force"]).is_err());
        assert!(Cli::try_parse_from(["tuxgate", "games", "record", "valorant"]).is_err());
    }
}
```

`crates/tuxgate-cli/src/commands.rs`:

```rs
//! Implementation of each subcommand.

use std::io::{BufRead, IsTerminal, Write};
use std::path::Path;

use clap::CommandFactory;
use serde::Serialize;
use tuxgate_bridge::{
    confirmation_matches, detect_windows, execute, plan_reboot, RebootMethod, RebootPlan,
    WindowsDetection, CONFIRMATION_PHRASE,
};
use tuxgate_compatibility::history::{Divergence, History, HistoryEntry, Regression, TestResult};
use tuxgate_compatibility::{Game, GameStatus, Source};
use tuxgate_core::finding::find;
use tuxgate_core::text::sanitize;
use tuxgate_core::time::UtcDateTime;
use tuxgate_core::{Finding, Status};
use tuxgate_diagnostics::readiness::attested_boot;
use tuxgate_reporting::render::{paint, status_tag};
use tuxgate_reporting::{render_text, write_report, CollectOptions, Format, Report, TextOptions};

use crate::app::{emit, emit_json, App, CliError, CliResult};
use crate::cli::{Cli, FormatArg, ResultArg};

const BOLD: &str = "1";
const DIM: &str = "2";

const NOT_TESTED: &str = "Statuses are documented from sources, not tested by Tuxgate.";

fn collect(app: &App, anonymize: bool) -> Report {
    Report::collect(
        &app.ctx,
        &app.compatibility,
        &CollectOptions {
            disabled_probes: &app.config.probes.disabled,
            anonymize,
            generated_at: UtcDateTime::now().iso8601(),
        },
    )
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- doctor

pub fn doctor(app: &App, verbose: bool) -> CliResult {
    let report = collect(app, false);
    if app.json {
        return emit_json(&report);
    }
    emit(&render_text(
        &report,
        &TextOptions {
            color: app.color,
            evidence: verbose,
        },
    ))
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- status

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
struct StatusOverview {
    tuxgate_version: &'static str,
    system: String,
    graphics: String,
    compatibility_layers: String,
    security: String,
    attested_boot: String,
    windows: String,
    riot_titles: String,
}

fn value_of(findings: &[Finding], id: &str) -> Option<String> {
    find(findings, id).and_then(|f| f.value.clone())
}

fn state_of(findings: &[Finding], id: &str, name: &str) -> String {
    match find(findings, id) {
        Some(finding) => match &finding.value {
            Some(value) => format!("{name} {value}"),
            None => format!("{name} {}", finding.status.label()),
        },
        None => format!("{name} not checked"),
    }
}

fn overview(report: &Report) -> StatusOverview {
    let findings = &report.findings;
    let unknown = || "unknown".to_string();

    let system = format!(
        "{}, kernel {}",
        value_of(findings, "system.distribution").unwrap_or_else(unknown),
        value_of(findings, "system.kernel").unwrap_or_else(unknown)
    );

    let gpus: Vec<String> = findings
        .iter()
        .filter(|f| f.id.starts_with("graphics.gpu"))
        .filter_map(|f| f.value.clone())
        .collect();
    let graphics = if gpus.is_empty() {
        "no graphics adapter identified".to_string()
    } else {
        gpus.join("; ")
    };

    let compatibility_layers = format!(
        "{}; {}",
        state_of(findings, "gaming.wine", "Wine"),
        match find(findings, "gaming.proton") {
            Some(f) if f.status == Status::Detected => "Proton installed".to_string(),
            Some(f) => format!("Proton {}", f.status.label()),
            None => "Proton not checked".to_string(),
        }
    );

    let security = [
        state_of(findings, "security.secure_boot", "Secure Boot"),
        state_of(findings, "security.tpm", "TPM:"),
        state_of(findings, "security.iommu", "IOMMU"),
        state_of(findings, "security.lockdown", "lockdown"),
    ]
    .join("; ");

    let mut counts: Vec<(GameStatus, usize)> = Vec::new();
    for game in &report.games {
        match counts.iter_mut().find(|(status, _)| *status == game.status) {
            Some((_, count)) => *count += 1,
            None => counts.push((game.status, 1)),
        }
    }
    let riot_titles = counts
        .iter()
        .map(|(status, count)| format!("{count} {}", status.label()))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join("; ");

    StatusOverview {
        tuxgate_version: report.tuxgate_version,
        system,
        graphics,
        compatibility_layers,
        security,
        attested_boot: format!(
            "{} of {} properties observed (informational, unrelated to Vanguard)",
            report.readiness.observed, report.readiness.total
        ),
        windows: report.windows.summary(),
        riot_titles,
    }
}

pub fn status(app: &App) -> CliResult {
    let report = collect(app, false);
    let overview = overview(&report);
    if app.json {
        return emit_json(&overview);
    }
    let label = |text: &str| paint(app.color, BOLD, &format!("{text:<15}"));
    let mut out = format!(
        "{}\n",
        paint(
            app.color,
            BOLD,
            &format!("Tuxgate {}", overview.tuxgate_version)
        )
    );
    for (name, value) in [
        ("System", &overview.system),
        ("Graphics", &overview.graphics),
        ("Wine / Proton", &overview.compatibility_layers),
        ("Security", &overview.security),
        ("Attested boot", &overview.attested_boot),
        ("Windows", &overview.windows),
        ("Riot titles", &overview.riot_titles),
    ] {
        out.push_str(&format!("{} {value}\n", label(name)));
    }
    out.push_str(&paint(
        app.color,
        DIM,
        "Run `tuxgate doctor` for details, `tuxgate check <game>` for one title.",
    ));
    emit(&out)
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------- games

pub fn games_list(app: &App) -> CliResult {
    let db = &app.compatibility;
    if app.json {
        return emit_json(&db.games);
    }
    let width = db.games.iter().map(|g| g.name.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
    let mut out = format!(
        "{}\n",
        paint(
            app.color,
            BOLD,
            &format!("Riot titles on Linux (database of {})", db.updated)
        )
    );
    for game in &db.games {
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "  {} {:<width$}  {}\n",
            status_tag(game.status.as_status(), app.color),
            game.name,
            game.status.label()
        ));
    }
    out.push_str(&format!("\n{}\n", paint(app.color, DIM, NOT_TESTED)));
    out.push_str(&paint(
        app.color,
        DIM,
        "Details and sources: tuxgate check <game>",
    ));
    emit(&out)
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
struct HistoryOutput<'a> {
    entries: Vec<&'a HistoryEntry>,
    regressions: Vec<Regression>,
    divergences: Vec<Divergence>,
}

fn divergences(app: &App, history: &History, only: Option<&Game>) -> Vec<Divergence> {
    app.compatibility
        .games
        .iter()
        .filter(|game| only.is_none_or(|o| o.id == game.id))
        .filter_map(|game| history.divergence(game))
        .collect()
}

fn describe_entry(entry: &HistoryEntry) -> String {
    let mut line = format!(
        "{}  {:<22} {}",
        entry.date,
        entry.game,
        entry.result.label()
    );
    if let Some(runner) = &entry.runner {
        line.push_str(&format!("  [{runner}]"));
    }
    if let Some(kernel) = &entry.kernel {
        line.push_str(&format!("  kernel {kernel}"));
    }
    if let Some(note) = &entry.note {
        line.push_str(&format!("  — {note}"));
    }
    line
}

fn describe_regression(regression: &Regression) -> String {
    format!(
        "{}: worked on {}, {} since {}",
        regression.game,
        regression.last_working.date,
        regression.first_failing.result.label(),
        regression.first_failing.date
    )
}

pub fn games_history(app: &App, game: Option<&str>) -> CliResult {
    let game = game.map(|q| app.compatibility.require(q)).transpose()?;
    let history = History::load(&app.require_paths()?.history_file())?;

    let entries: Vec<&HistoryEntry> = match game {
        Some(game) => history.for_game(&game.id),
        None => history.entries.iter().collect(),
    };
    let regressions: Vec<Regression> = match game {
        Some(game) => history.regression(&game.id).into_iter().collect(),
        None => history.regressions(),
    };
    let divergences = divergences(app, &history, game);

    if app.json {
        return emit_json(&HistoryOutput {
            entries,
            regressions,
            divergences,
        });
    }

    let mut out = String::new();
    if entries.is_empty() {
        out.push_str("No test has been recorded yet.\n");
        out.push_str(
            "Record one with: tuxgate games record <game> --result works|broken|blocked\n",
        );
    } else {
        out.push_str(&paint(app.color, BOLD, "Your recorded tests"));
        out.push('\n');
        for entry in &entries {
            out.push_str(&format!("  {}\n", describe_entry(entry)));
        }
    }
    if !regressions.is_empty() {
        out.push_str(&format!("\n{}\n", paint(app.color, BOLD, "Regressions")));
        for regression in &regressions {
            out.push_str(&format!("  {}\n", describe_regression(regression)));
        }
    }
    if !divergences.is_empty() {
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "\n{}\n",
            paint(app.color, BOLD, "Differences with the database")
        ));
        for divergence in &divergences {
            out.push_str(&format!("  {}: {}\n", divergence.game, divergence.message));
        }
    }
    out.push_str(&format!(
        "\n{}",
        paint(
            app.color,
            DIM,
            "These records are yours and stay on this machine."
        )
    ));
    emit(&out)
}

pub fn games_record(
    app: &App,
    game: &str,
    result: ResultArg,
    runner: Option<String>,
    note: Option<String>,
) -> CliResult {
    let game = app.compatibility.require(game)?;
    let path = app.require_paths()?.history_file();
    let mut history = History::load(&path)?;

    let entry = HistoryEntry {
        game: game.id.clone(),
        date: UtcDateTime::now().date(),
        result: match result {
            ResultArg::Works => TestResult::Works,
            ResultArg::Broken => TestResult::Broken,
            ResultArg::Blocked => TestResult::Blocked,
        },
        kernel: app
            .ctx
            .read_trimmed("/proc/sys/kernel/osrelease")
            .ok()
            .filter(|k| !k.is_empty()),
        runner: runner.map(|r| sanitize(r.trim())).filter(|r| !r.is_empty()),
        note: note.map(|n| sanitize(n.trim())).filter(|n| !n.is_empty()),
        tuxgate_version: tuxgate_core::VERSION.to_string(),
    };
    history.record(entry.clone())?;
    history.save(&path)?;

    if app.json {
        return emit_json(&entry);
    }
    let mut out = format!(
        "Recorded: {} is \"{}\" on {}.\nSaved to {}\n",
        game.name,
        entry.result.label(),
        entry.date,
        path.display()
    );
    if let Some(regression) = history.regression(&game.id) {
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "Regression: {}\n",
            describe_regression(&regression)
        ));
    }
    if let Some(divergence) = history.divergence(game) {
        out.push_str(&format!("Note: {}\n", divergence.message));
    }
    emit(&out)
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------- check

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
struct CitedSource<'a> {
    claim: &'a str,
    #[serde(flatten)]
    source: &'a Source,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
struct CheckOutput<'a> {
    game: &'a Game,
    sources: Vec<CitedSource<'a>>,
    /// The status as it applies to this system.
    system_status: Status,
    verdict: String,
    relevant_findings: Vec<&'a Finding>,
    windows: &'a WindowsDetection,
    latest_record: Option<HistoryEntry>,
    divergence: Option<Divergence>,
}

fn verdict(game: &Game) -> String {
    match game.status {
        GameStatus::Blocked => format!(
            "{} cannot run on this system. No setting on this machine changes that: the \
             requirement is set by Riot Games.",
            game.name
        ),
        GameStatus::NotApplicable => format!("{} is not a desktop title.", game.name),
        GameStatus::ReportedWorking | GameStatus::MixedReports | GameStatus::ReportedBroken => {
            format!(
                "Tuxgate cannot tell whether {} will run here: it has not been tested by this \
                 project, and Riot does not support it on Linux.",
                game.name
            )
        }
        GameStatus::Unknown => format!("Nothing reliable is known about {} on Linux.", game.name),
    }
}

fn relevant_ids(status: GameStatus) -> &'static [&'static str] {
    match status {
        // For a blocked title the only local facts worth showing are the
        // ones people ask about. None of them changes the outcome.
        GameStatus::Blocked => &["security.virtualization", "gaming.wine", "gaming.proton"],
        GameStatus::NotApplicable => &[],
        _ => &[
            "graphics.vulkan_runtime",
            "graphics.vulkan_drivers",
            "gaming.wine",
            "gaming.proton",
        ],
    }
}

pub fn check(app: &App, query: &str) -> CliResult {
    let db = &app.compatibility;
    let game = db.require(query)?;
    let findings = tuxgate_diagnostics::run_all(&app.ctx, &app.config.probes.disabled);
    let windows = detect_windows(&app.ctx);
    let readiness = attested_boot(&findings);

    let history = match &app.paths {
        Some(paths) => History::load(&paths.history_file())?,
        None => History::new(),
    };
    let latest_record = history.latest(&game.id).cloned();
    let divergence = history.divergence(game);

    let sources: Vec<CitedSource<'_>> = game
        .evidence
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|evidence| {
            db.source(&evidence.source).map(|source| CitedSource {
                claim: &evidence.claim,
                source,
            })
        })
        .collect();
    let relevant: Vec<&Finding> = relevant_ids(game.status)
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|id| find(&findings, id))
        .collect();

    if app.json {
        return emit_json(&CheckOutput {
            game,
            sources,
            system_status: game.status.as_status(),
            verdict: verdict(game),
            relevant_findings: relevant,
            windows: &windows,
            latest_record,
            divergence,
        });
    }

    let color = app.color;
    let mut out = format!("{}\n", paint(color, BOLD, &game.name));
    out.push_str(&format!("  Status:      {}\n", game.status.label()));
    out.push_str(&format!("  Basis:       {}\n", game.basis.label()));
    if let Some(anti_cheat) = &game.anti_cheat {
        out.push_str(&format!("  Anti-cheat:  {anti_cheat}\n"));
    }
    out.push_str(&format!("\n  {}\n", game.summary));

    if !game.details.is_empty() {
        out.push_str(&format!("\n{}\n", paint(color, BOLD, "Why")));
        for detail in &game.details {
            out.push_str(&format!("  - {detail}\n"));
        }
    }
    if !game.ways_to_play.is_empty() {
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "\n{}\n",
            paint(color, BOLD, "Legitimate ways to play today")
        ));
        for way in &game.ways_to_play {
            out.push_str(&format!("  - {way}\n"));
        }
    }
    if !sources.is_empty() {
        out.push_str(&format!("\n{}\n", paint(color, BOLD, "Sources")));
        for cited in &sources {
            let source = cited.source;
            let date = source.date.as_deref().unwrap_or("undated");
            out.push_str(&format!(
                "  [{}] grade {}, {date} — {}: {}\n",
                source.id,
                source.grade.label(),
                source.publisher,
                source.title
            ));
            out.push_str(&format!("       {}\n", cited.claim));
            out.push_str(&format!("       {}\n", paint(color, DIM, &source.url)));
        }
    }

    out.push_str(&format!("\n{}\n", paint(color, BOLD, "On this system")));
    out.push_str(&format!(
        "  {} {}\n",
        status_tag(game.status.as_status(), color),
        verdict(game)
    ));
    for finding in &relevant {
        let value = finding.value.as_deref().unwrap_or("");
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "  {} {}{}{value}\n",
            status_tag(finding.status, color),
            finding.title,
            if value.is_empty() { "" } else { ": " }
        ));
    }
    if game.status == GameStatus::Blocked {
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "  {} {}\n",
            status_tag(windows.status, color),
            windows.summary()
        ));
        if windows.status == Status::Detected {
            out.push_str("      `tuxgate windows reboot` can restart into Windows once.\n");
        }
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "  {}\n",
            paint(
                color,
                DIM,
                &format!(
                    "Attested-boot model: {} of {} properties observed. Informational only; \
                     it has no effect on Vanguard.",
                    readiness.observed, readiness.total
                )
            )
        ));
    }

    if let Some(record) = &latest_record {
        out.push_str(&format!("\n{}\n", paint(color, BOLD, "Your latest record")));
        out.push_str(&format!("  {}\n", describe_entry(record)));
    }
    if let Some(divergence) = &divergence {
        out.push_str(&format!("  {}\n", divergence.message));
    }
    out.push_str(&format!("\n{}", paint(color, DIM, NOT_TESTED)));
    emit(&out)
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- report

pub fn report(
    app: &App,
    format: FormatArg,
    output: Option<&Path>,
    no_anonymize: bool,
    force_anonymize: bool,
    force: bool,
) -> CliResult {
    let anonymize = force_anonymize || (!no_anonymize && app.config.report.anonymize);
    let format = if app.json {
        Format::Json
    } else {
        match format {
            FormatArg::Text => Format::Text,
            FormatArg::Markdown => Format::Markdown,
            FormatArg::Json => Format::Json,
        }
    };

    let report = collect(app, anonymize);
    let rendered = report.render(format)?;

    let Some(path) = output else {
        // No destination: this is the preview. Nothing is written.
        return emit(&rendered);
    };

    write_report(path, &rendered, force)?;
    let privacy = if anonymize {
        "anonymised"
    } else {
        "NOT anonymised: it contains user paths"
    };
    eprintln!("Report written to {} ({privacy}).", path.display());
    eprintln!("Read it before sharing it. Tuxgate has not sent it anywhere.");
    Ok(())
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------- windows

pub fn windows_detect(app: &App) -> CliResult {
    let detection = detect_windows(&app.ctx);
    if app.json {
        return emit_json(&detection);
    }
    let color = app.color;
    let mut out = format!(
        "{} {}\n",
        status_tag(detection.status, color),
        detection.summary()
    );
    for entry in &detection.efi_entries {
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "  Firmware entry Boot{}: {}{}\n",
            entry.label(),
            entry.description,
            if entry.active { "" } else { " (disabled)" }
        ));
    }
    if let Some(id) = &detection.systemd_boot_entry {
        out.push_str(&format!("  systemd-boot entry: {id}\n"));
    }
    if let Some(path) = &detection.esp_loader {
        out.push_str(&format!("  Boot manager file: {path}\n"));
    }
    if !detection.partition_hints.is_empty() {
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "  Partitions that may belong to Windows: {}\n",
            detection.partition_hints.join(", ")
        ));
    }
    for note in &detection.notes {
        out.push_str(&format!("  {}\n", paint(color, DIM, &format!("- {note}"))));
    }
    out.push_str(&paint(
        color,
        DIM,
        "Detection is read-only. Nothing was changed.",
    ));
    emit(&out)
}

fn describe_plan(plan: &RebootPlan, color: bool) -> String {
    let mut out = format!(
        "{}\n",
        paint(color, BOLD, "Plan: restart into Windows once")
    );
    let method = match &plan.method {
        RebootMethod::SystemdBootEntry { entry_id } => {
            format!("systemd-boot one-time entry \"{entry_id}\"")
        }
        RebootMethod::EfiBootNext {
            boot_number,
            description,
        } => format!("UEFI BootNext, firmware entry Boot{boot_number} \"{description}\""),
    };
    out.push_str(&format!(
        "  Method: {method}\n\n  Commands, in this order:\n"
    ));
    for (index, command) in plan.commands.iter().enumerate() {
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "    {}. {}{}\n       {}\n",
            index + 1,
            command.display(),
            if command.elevated {
                "   (asks for administrator rights)"
            } else {
                ""
            },
            paint(color, DIM, &command.purpose)
        ));
    }
    out.push('\n');
    for warning in &plan.warnings {
        out.push_str(&format!("  ! {warning}\n"));
    }
    out
}

pub fn windows_reboot(app: &App, dry_run: bool) -> CliResult {
    if app.json && !dry_run {
        return Err(CliError(
            "--json cannot be combined with an actual reboot; add --dry-run to print the plan"
                .to_string(),
        ));
    }

    let detection = detect_windows(&app.ctx);
    let plan = plan_reboot(&app.ctx, &detection)?;

    if app.json {
        return emit_json(&plan);
    }
    emit(&describe_plan(&plan, app.color))?;

    if dry_run {
        return emit("Dry run: nothing was executed.");
    }

    // The confirmation must come from a person at a terminal. There is
    // deliberately no flag to skip it.
    let stdin = std::io::stdin();
    if !stdin.is_terminal() {
        return Err(CliError(
            "confirmation requires an interactive terminal; nothing was done. \
             Use --dry-run to only display the plan."
                .to_string(),
        ));
    }

    print!("Type \"{CONFIRMATION_PHRASE}\" to confirm, anything else to cancel: ");
    std::io::stdout().flush()?;
    let mut answer = String::new();
    stdin.lock().read_line(&mut answer)?;

    if !confirmation_matches(&answer) {
        return emit("Cancelled. Nothing was changed.");
    }

    execute(&app.ctx, &plan)?;
    emit("Restart requested.")
}

// ------------------------------------------------------- sources, probes

pub fn sources(app: &App) -> CliResult {
    let db = &app.compatibility;
    if app.json {
        return emit_json(&db.sources);
    }
    let mut out = format!(
        "{}\n",
        paint(
            app.color,
            BOLD,
            &format!("Sources (consulted {})", db.sources_accessed)
        )
    );
    for source in &db.sources {
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "  {:<4} {:<2}  {:<10}  {}: {}\n        {}\n",
            source.id,
            source.grade.label(),
            source.date.as_deref().unwrap_or("undated"),
            source.publisher,
            source.title,
            paint(app.color, DIM, &source.url)
        ));
    }
    out.push_str(&paint(
        app.color,
        DIM,
        "Grades: A primary, read directly; A- primary, not read directly; B secondary; C community.",
    ));
    emit(&out)
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
struct ProbeInfo {
    id: &'static str,
    description: &'static str,
    enabled: bool,
}

pub fn probes(app: &App) -> CliResult {
    let probes: Vec<ProbeInfo> = tuxgate_diagnostics::default_registry()
        .describe()
        .into_iter()
        .map(|(id, description)| ProbeInfo {
            id,
            description,
            enabled: !app.config.probes.disabled.iter().any(|d| d == id),
        })
        .collect();
    if app.json {
        return emit_json(&probes);
    }
    let mut out = format!("{}\n", paint(app.color, BOLD, "Diagnostic probes"));
    for probe in &probes {
        out.push_str(&format!(
            "  {:<16} {:<9} {}\n",
            probe.id,
            if probe.enabled { "enabled" } else { "disabled" },
            probe.description
        ));
    }
    out.push_str(&paint(
        app.color,
        DIM,
        "Disable a probe in ~/.config/tuxgate/config.toml: [probes] disabled = [\"<id>\"]",
    ));
    emit(&out)
}

// ------------------------------------------------- completions, man page

pub fn completions(shell: clap_complete::Shell) -> CliResult {
    let mut buffer = Vec::new();
    clap_complete::generate(shell, &mut Cli::command(), "tuxgate", &mut buffer);
    emit(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&buffer))
}

pub fn man() -> CliResult {
    let mut buffer = Vec::new();
    clap_mangen::Man::new(Cli::command()).render(&mut buffer)?;
    emit(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&buffer))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use tuxgate_compatibility::Compatibility;
    use tuxgate_core::testing::{scenarios, Scenario};
    use tuxgate_core::Config;

    fn app_for(scenario: &Scenario) -> App {
        App {
            ctx: scenario.context(),
            config: Config::default(),
            compatibility: Compatibility::builtin().expect("database"),
            paths: None,
            json: false,
            color: false,
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn overview_summarises_a_gaming_desktop() {
        let scenario = scenarios::cachyos_desktop();
        let app = app_for(&scenario);
        let overview = overview(&collect(&app, false));
        assert_eq!(
            overview.system,
            "CachyOS (rolling), kernel 6.17.9-2-cachyos"
        );
        assert_eq!(overview.graphics, "NVIDIA [10de:2684], driver: nvidia");
        assert!(overview.compatibility_layers.contains("Wine wine-10.19"));
        assert!(overview.compatibility_layers.contains("Proton installed"));
        assert!(overview.security.contains("Secure Boot enabled"));
        assert!(overview.security.contains("TPM: TPM 2.0"));
        assert!(overview.security.contains("lockdown none"));
        assert!(overview.attested_boot.starts_with("5 of 8"));
        assert!(overview.windows.contains("Windows installation found"));
        assert!(overview
            .riot_titles
            .contains("4 blocked by an external requirement"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn overview_copes_with_a_bare_system() {
        let scenario = scenarios::vm_guest();
        let app = app_for(&scenario);
        let overview = overview(&collect(&app, false));
        assert!(overview.security.contains("Secure Boot not applicable"));
        assert!(overview.security.contains("TPM: not detected"));
        assert!(overview.windows.contains("could not be determined"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn verdicts_never_promise_that_a_game_runs() {
        let db = Compatibility::builtin().expect("database");
        for game in &db.games {
            let text = verdict(game);
            assert!(!text.contains("will run on"), "{}", game.id);
            assert!(!text.to_lowercase().contains("compatible"), "{}", game.id);
            if game.status == GameStatus::Blocked {
                assert!(text.contains("cannot run"), "{}", game.id);
                assert!(text.contains("set by Riot Games"), "{}", game.id);
            }
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn plan_description_lists_commands_and_warnings() {
        let scenario = scenarios::cachyos_desktop();
        let ctx = scenario.context();
        let plan = plan_reboot(&ctx, &detect_windows(&ctx)).expect("plan");
        let text = describe_plan(&plan, false);
        assert!(text.contains("systemd-boot one-time entry \"auto-windows\""));
        assert!(text.contains("1. /usr/bin/systemctl reboot --boot-loader-entry=auto-windows"));
        assert!(text.contains("! The computer restarts immediately."));
    }
}
```
